- **why-this-logic**: Trace logic back to its source, or a requirement forward to its code and tests. Backward - pointed at a condition, value, filter, mapping or default in code, a ticket or a document: one row per decision point, each with its source, one proof mark (cited / told / measured) and one verdict (matches / contradicts / no source / cannot check from the document); writes docs/why/&lt;thing&gt;.md. Forward - pointed at a spec section, acceptance criteria, a requirement sentence or an R-ID: each statement gets an ID and a status (implemented / untested / not found / source changed) in docs/why/requirements.m… ([ThodsaphonSonthiphin/workflow-daily-work](https://github.com/ThodsaphonSonthiphin/workflow-daily-work/tree/HEAD/skills/why-this-logic) · 2 copies)
- **why-token**: Token launch advisory for founders. Forcing questions that surface whether a token makes sense, how value accrues, which parties are needed, and what the realistic timeline to TGE looks like. Produces a structured document founders can share with co-founders, advisors, or legal counsel. Use when a founder says "should I launch a token", "how do I launch a token", "tokenomics", "TGE planning", "token design", or "I want to create a token". ([ckpxgfnksd-max/tls](https://github.com/ckpxgfnksd-max/tls/tree/HEAD/tls-why-token) · ★8)
- **why-tree**: Diagnose a hard problem by building an evidence-graded "Why Tree" - a Goldratt Current-Reality Tree that fans many AI agents across the problem's branch-space, grades every node by the kind of evidence behind it (validating measured numbers' pipelines before trusting them), tries to refute its own load-bearing branches, and converges on the ONE system constraint plus the cheapest test that would prove it. Use when someone asks "why is X happening / why are we falling short of a goal", wants a root-cause analysis with real rigor (not a 5-minute 5-Whys), needs to find the real bottleneck before… ([BayramAnnakov/systems-thinking-skills](https://github.com/BayramAnnakov/systems-thinking-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/why-tree) · ★29)

- **whydunit**: 东野圭吾风格悬疑改写器。把一个已有的故事改写成东野式的追问型悬疑结构，可直接产出完整推理小说正文。不是加尸体，是重新分配信息——找出文本里已经存在的隐瞒，把它藏得更晚，换一个不知情的人来讲。当用户提到把某篇课文/小说/故事/新闻/剧本改成悬疑、改成推理、改成侦探小说、东野圭吾风格改写、叙事视角重构、悬念重排、whydunit、追问化时使用。用户说"把《孔乙己》改成悬疑""这个故事能不能变成推理小说""用东野的写法重写这段""帮我做个叙事结构改造"都应触发。调用时先让用户四选一：改造方案／侦探小说／带音效的广播剧剧本／听书版音频。也适用于语文教学中的叙事结构拆解任务、反向操作（把悬疑结构拆平以验证规则）。用户说"做成有声版""生成听书""写成广播剧""配音稿"时同样触发。 ([Jimmy-ai-studio/dongye-detective-skill](https://github.com/Jimmy-ai-studio/dongye-detective-skill/tree/HEAD/whydunit) · ★1)
